X X . X . . X . . . .` · PROBE `. . . . . . . . . . . .` TWELVE OF TWELVE · REVERTED identical byte for byte.** The probe is REVERTED and nothing of it is on origin — `gc_heap.c` is the collector, a shared node, so it is an **ASK to the cfo with the measurement**. ⛔ **WHAT I HAD WRONG, and it was the prose, which is this row's own subject matter:** the census docstring said the walk visits the below-base words *"as UNTYPED WORDS … never visited as a root"* and arm (f) told the next reader *"THE CURE IS THE PLANNER'S"*. **`gc_walk_words` tries `gc_cell_visit` on every below-base word FIRST and counts only what that recognizer REFUSES — so a store below the region base is NECESSARY BUT NOT SUFFICIENT for a lost value**, exactly as the cfo had refuted twice from the other side (CFO-114/CFO-136: a negative offset is where spine words live BY CONSTRUCTION). ⛔ **The sharper lesson is mine and I am not softening it: I READ `gc_cell_visit` EARLY IN THE SITTING, SAW `DT_X` WAS ABSENT, AND SET IT ASIDE BECAUSE THE BATON FRAMED THE ROW AS THE PLANNER'S.** The evidence was in my hand before hq_snobol4 published and the frame I had been handed is what stopped me looking at it. ⭐ **EVERY NUMBER SURVIVES UNCHANGED (162 members / 930 shielded stores / 294 undecidable); only the MEANING is corrected** — a member is *a safe point whose correctness rests on a tag-recognizer heuristic instead of on the frame map § 3 says should cover it*. **When the recognizer fix lands, arm (f) goes green with the members still at 162, and that reading — green band, 162 unmapped stores — is precisely the residual this row exists to remove.** The row is better posed after the correction than before it.

- **CTO-93 LANDED — rbx STEP 3: THE SIX BLOB PINS ARE DECLARED IN CODE, AND THE FLIP'S PREMISE IS MEASURED FALSE (2026-09-20 13:xx CDT, cto; MODE SEPTET; SCRIP `fffe99658`).** ⭐ **A REGISTER PLANE NEEDS AN ASSERT, NOT A PAGE:** the architecture has read *rbx = arena heap top* the whole time, and rbx still drifted into scratch when the no-modes law deleted the `ZC_PORT_*` HEAP arm that owned it — because a plane written in a table and enforced nowhere has no failure mode. r9 held the same years and the only mechanical difference is one `_Static_assert`. Five more now exist (`PIN_FRONTIER_REG`, `PIN_CAS_TOP_REG`, `PIN_SIGMA_REG`, `PIN_CURSOR_REG`, `PIN_SUBJLEN_REG`), each naming its register, its role and the sites that read it; **proven to fail once** — renaming the subject pin to r12 fails the build at `x86_asm.h:360` with the assert's own message. The gate `test_gate_gc_rbx_is_the_frontier.sh` (wired blocking, adopted into the wiring floor) keeps step 1's claim: every rbx spelling in the templates and the emitter is in a declared set of three, a fourth reds, no template spells the push-rbx saver, and `FAIL_ONCE=1` plants a scratch use and is seen. The emitted output is **byte-identical** (20 Icon benchmark artifacts unchanged, 0 updated), which is the honest claim for a landing that declares rather than changes. ⛔ **THE FLIP CANNOT BE DELIVERED AS THE ROW WRITES IT, AND THE REASON IS A MEASUREMENT: EMITTED CODE NEVER CALLS THE ALLOCATOR.** Zero mentions of `rt_gcheap_alloc`, `rt_ws_alloc`, `rt_heap_alloc`, `rt_pm_struct_alloc` or `rt_agg_alloc` in `src/templates` or `src/emitter`; the safe-point census reads 244 allocating call sites in emitted code and every one is a call into a runtime entry that allocates internally (1655 of 8645 runtime functions reach the allocator); the GOT load the row wants gone lives in `rtx_alloc.s` 7/46/60 and `rtx_str.s` 100, inside the runtime. So no emitted instruction reads or writes the frontier, a cache of it has no consumer, and the arm that reads the emitted `.s` for an advancing rbx presumes a path that does not exist. A cache only pays where the consumer shares the register world: the runtime may hold the frontier in rbx across its own internals because SysV preserves it, but every call from emitted code arrives with the emitted world's rbx and must save and restore it, which costs exactly the memory round trip the cache was to remove. Two designs are with the ceo — an emitted inline fast path for compile-time-constant allocation shapes with the write-back/reload discipline (the real prize, and the allocator's fast path then written twice and held equal by a gate), or defer the flip to its own row with a written design, which is my recommendation because it is a speed win and not a correctness fix while the acceptance path still holds hq_prolog's findall red.
- **CTO-92 LANDED — rbx STEP 1: EVERY SCRATCH USE IS OFF THE REGISTER, AND THE FIFTH SITE WAS DEAD CODE (2026-09-20 08:xx CDT, cto; MODE SEPTET; row `gc-rbx-is-the-bump-frontier-...`, ceo CEO-959/974; Lon: *"RBX was always meant to be the GC bump frontier. If it is not so, make it so."*; SCRIP `25d0955d8`, corpus `9a69dfcc2`).** ⭐ **A RESTORATION, NOT A NEW DESIGN:** the frontier lived in the HEAP arm of the `ZC_PORT_*` selector; when the no-modes law deleted those arms the register plane went with them, and rbx — preserved by SysV and therefore free-looking — was picked up as scratch by four live sites. **THE ALIGNED-CALL SAVER NEEDS NO CALLEE-SAVED REGISTER AT ALL:** one shared pair of helpers (`x86_align_call_enter`/`x86_align_call_leave`) parks the caller's rsp ON the aligned stack, so rsp is 16-aligned at the call as the ABI requires and the restore works on every path that jumps to the epilogue; r11 holds the parked pointer for three instructions before a call that clobbers it anyway. The two sites that READ through the saver re-base on r11 with every offset shifted 8 down, and the four scan boxes take r11 for a shuffle the preceding call has already clobbered. ⛔ **THE FIFTH SITE THE CENSUS NAMED DOES NOT EXIST IN THE LIVE TREE:** `emit_aligned_call_rt` had a declaration, a definition and ZERO callers — DELETED under the dead-code rule, which is also why its hand-encoded binary arm never needed re-encoding. **MEASURED:** the emitted asm of an Icon scan witness carries zero rbx; the three trace-exercising Icon master entries match their refs on stdout in both media and their `&trace` streams agree with each other and byte-for-byte with icont's own (the arm that actually grades the re-basing); the seven smokes 0 FAIL; the GC witness battery 104 of 108 in BOTH media at the tiny arena (the four = hq_prolog's findall row); `make test-arena` 43 arms 2 red, both pre-existing and attributed; the sno-chain gate 8/8; preflight 56/0; 20 of 23 Icon benchmark artifacts regenerated, 3522 insertions against 3522 deletions, one shape twice per site; **IcnM at 1 MB m3 824/2/0 m4 825/1/0 and SnoM m3 1963/2/0 m4 1963/2/0 — not one entry moved from the two landings before this one.** NAMED AND NOT MINE: `test_gate_picker_lane_table_agrees_with_mode.sh` went red at the SEPTET flip and was red on origin under the same reading; the ceo cured it at `5367103df` while this landing was in verdict.
- **CTO-91 LANDED — THE THIRD CRASH CLASS: A CHAIN ENTERED FROM C MID-MATCH NOW INHERITS THE LIVE MATCH (2026-09-20 00:xx CDT, cto; MODE QUARTET; row `sno-chain-entry-dumps-core-in-both-modes-on-the-mid-match-witness-a-third-crash-class`, CEO-961/942; SCRIP `2ce1d8a74`, corpus `9a7a3db52`/`eb1eff6d7`).** ⭐⭐ **TWO REGISTER-PLANE HOLES UNDER ONE CORE DUMP, each named at its faulting site with gdb rather than reasoned at.** The gate's mid-match witness (an outer match holding a pending capture `X`, a deferred `*EVAL("PLAIN()")` whose body runs its own match with a replacement, then a pending `Y`) dumped core in BOTH media at BOTH arenas on origin/main `4e6aaa856` and on the E tree alike, where the oracle prints `X=[AB] Y=[CD]`. **(1) THE SUBJECT.** `rt_defer_close` read through the parked subject `Σ == NULL` while the box's own r13/r15 still held `"ABPCD"`/5: a chain entered from C by `rt_chain_enter`/`rt_chain_enter_v` inherits C's r13/r15, where they are ordinary callee-saved registers, and the inner match's head record saved them and its end restored `Σ`/`Σlen` from them. The two trampolines now seed r13 from `Σ` and r15 from `Σlen` — the live outer subject mid-match, NULL/0 when cold — the same both-directions argument they already made for r12 against the dcap base. With that alone the crash MOVED rather than went: rc=0 at the shipped arena printing `X=[]` (the capture lost), rc=139 at 1 MB inside `rt_dcap_pump` on a corrupt entry. **(2) THE PEND TOP, and this is the half the row was named for.** The trampolines keep r12 only when it already lies inside the dcap island and otherwise seed it from the cell at `RT_DCAP_TOP`; traced at `rt_chain_enter_v`, r12 was C's `0x68`, so the seed came from the cell — which held the BASE, written once at init and never tracking the live top — and the inner match wrote its mark there, over the outer's pending `X` (the pump dump read the outer frame's first entry as varname `""` delta 2 where `X` delta 0 had been). The box now PARKS r12 into that cell before the three defer calls that can evaluate a chain (`rt_defer_probe_run`, `rt_defer_open_entry`, `rt_patv_defer_open_entry`) and after every match end's reset of r12, so the cell IS the live pend top whenever C runs and the base at rest, and every seed from it — the trampolines' cold arm, `rt_outer_call` — is right in both directions. **VERDICT ON THE LANDED TREE:** the witness prints `X=[AB] Y=[CD]` in both media at 512 MB and at 1 MB; the row's gate `test_gate_sno_chain_entry_establishes_the_pend_top_cold_and_preserves_it_mid_match.sh` **PASS=8 FAIL=0** (4 witnesses × 2 modes) plain and at `SCRIP_GC_STRESS=3`, both under `SCRIP_HEAP_MB=1`; the GC witness battery 104 of 108 in BOTH media (the four = `hb_pl_root_cells`, the ceo's findall row); the E DONE-WHEN gate, register-resident, reporter, decidable and safe-point-shape gates all PASS; preflight 56/0, strip_comments 0, no implicit declaration, the seven smokes 0 FAIL; the benchmark (4) and demo (18) `.s` artifacts regenerated and committed; **SnoM at 1 MB m3 1963/2/0 and m4 1963/2/0 — byte-identical to the E landing's reading, not one entry moved.**

F3's surviving "return-PC key" wording is the same staleness one page down. **NEXT:** widen the gate's witnesses from three frontends to seven (a divergence in Snocone/Raku/Pascal/Rebus would not be seen today, and under ONE RUNNER no seat may reach for a board to cover that); then the `reserved` quad — F1 requires the map to carry the pointer-bearing callee-saved registers and nothing reads that quad yet; and the remaining 208 poll sites.

- **CTO-43 (2026-09-13 CDT, cto; MODE NONET; lane: PROLOG + THE FRAME) — A BYRD BOX HAS NO DESTRUCTOR PORT, SO α OVERWROTE THE ONLY POINTER TO A SUSPENDED COEXPRESSION. SCRIP `3b654c129` + corpus `d0a028479` + .github `b676a1f5e` = origin/main; FINDING `FINDING-2026-09-13-cto-a-byrd-box-has-no-destructor-port-so-alpha-overwrote-the-only-pointer-to-a-suspended-coexpression.md`; gate `test_gate_abandoned_generator_handle_is_dropped_at_alpha.sh` (18 arms, m3+m4) wired into `make test`.** (1) **THE DEFECT**: `bb_call_value`'s α cleared its generator-handle word with a blind `mov FRQ(H), 0`. A **bounded** caller — one that takes a result and never backs into the box — leaves a live genp handle there, and the next α of the same box, same frame, same word, wrote 0 over the only pointer to a **suspended pthread**. Icon `every i := 1 to n do x := p()` with `p` a procedure value naming a generator: **11.2 kB and one live thread per abandoned call**, dead linear, SIGABRT in `pthread_create` at n=60000. After: **0.40 kB/iter, n=60000 completes**, both modes. (2) **⭐ THE STRUCTURAL STATEMENT, WHICH OUTLIVES THE CURE**: the four ports are α β γ ω and **none of them means "this box is finished"** — ω means *fail onward* and a bounded caller never reaches it, it simply stops asking. So **the next α of the same box is the only moment the runtime can observe that an incarnation is over**, and that was precisely the instruction destroying the evidence. Any future resource a box parks in its own frame inherits this. It is **CEO-711 from the caller's side** and belongs in hq_U's ONE LIFETIME MODEL with CEO-690/691. (3) **hq_R's ask answered and RENAMED**: they asked for `rt_pl_goal_drop_h`; landed as **`rt_proc_drop_frame_h(void **hslot)`**, the language-blind peer of `rt_proc_resume_frame_h` — a `rt_pl_` prefix on a shared runtime sink is the language-identity leak RULES.md calls a bug, not a style choice. Unblocks **24 cases across two hq_R rows**. Total by construction via `rt_genp_lookup`, so the Icon spine flag `1` and a retained PL callee frame base miss the list and are zeroed exactly as before. (4) **THE CONTROL ARMS ARE THE HALF WORTH KEEPING**: under the reverted compiler `abandon` went RED in both modes while `exhaust` (a generator driven to failure) and `det` (a deterministic callee) stayed GREEN — a gate watching total RSS would have tripped on any of the three. (5) **⛔ A RESIDUAL NAMED, NOT ABSORBED**: `abandon` and `exhaust` now read the *same* 0.39–0.40 kB/iter slope; the exhausted path never abandons a handle, so it **cannot be this class** and I do not claim it. Fenced at 2.0 so it cannot grow unowned. (6) **⛔ MY OWN CORRECTION, SECOND APPLICATION OF "A CORRECTION MUST TRAVEL AS FAR AS THE ERROR DID" AT THIS SEAT TODAY**: I told hq_P their witness E had gone from a loud failure to a silent one. ## THE CTO LOOP
1. Session start as every seat: `fetch` + `ff-only` merge of SCRIP, corpus, .github; `bash SCRIP/scripts/s4e_msg.sh check`; print the banner (`python3 .github/scripts/util_suite_banner.py`).
2. Resume the claimed row (`s4e_msg.sh next` resumes your own claim). One problem at a time; the ceo names the next from the suite table when this one lands (the class blocking the most package programs across lanes).
3. ASM-DIFF-FIRST (RULES.md § Debugging order), the three zetas (ARCH-ENGINE.md), zero comments, LF, `-O0`, no new globals without Lon's banner grant, `make` incremental for the verdict.
4. Push per rung with the ledger line before the next step — the account change is abrupt (ARCH-FLEET-CEO.md § TWO OPERATING MODES, CEO-324); nothing lives only in this session.
5. Land: rebase, re-prove, push code repos then `.github`; FINDING named for the claim; move this LIVE CURSOR; `s4e_msg.sh done <topic>` (computed); telegram `ceo` with the hash.
6. The CTO does not audit, arbitrate, rank, or write law. Escalations go to `ceo/inbox`; Lon's in-chat word wins immediately and is routed into this file the same session.
